## `AFG-9700-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Infant Bottle Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Household Food Sanitizer Pesticide Residue & Baby Bottle Sterility
- **Intro:** Infant Bottle Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Household Food Sanitizer Pesticide Residue & Baby Bottle Sterility — Pre-Calibration Inspection and PPE Donning: Gasket must have no tears or deformation; all electrical cords must have no exposed wires; floor must be dry (no standing water).
